TL;DR: 本研究系统探讨了激活导向的人格特征在教育生成和评分中的影响，发现人格导向会降低答案质量，尤其是在开放性的英语语言艺术任务中影响更大；同时，评分者的人格特质会导致可预测的评分偏差，且不同任务和模型架构对此敏感度不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于激活的人格导向在教育场景下的影响，特别是在短答案生成和自动评分中的效果，以了解其潜在风险与挑战。

Method: 研究使用ASAP-SAS基准数据集，针对七种人格特质，在三种不同架构的大语言模型上进行实验，分析人格向量对答案生成质量和自动评分结果的影响。

Result: 人格导向整体降低了答案质量，对开放式英语语言艺术（ELA）任务的影响是事实性科学任务的11倍；评分方面，负面人格（如邪恶、无礼）导致更严厉评分，正面人格（如善良、乐观）则更宽容；ELA任务比科学任务更容易受评分者个性化影响（2.5-3倍），MoE模型的校准偏移约为密集模型的6倍。

Conclusion: 在教育场景中部署人格导向模型时，需考虑任务类型和模型架构的差异，实施任务感知和架构感知的校准策略，以减轻不必要的偏差。

Abstract: Activation-based steering can personalize large language models at inference time, but its effects in educational settings remain unclear. We study persona vectors for seven character traits in short-answer generation and automated scoring on the ASAP-SAS benchmark across three models spanning two architectures. Persona steering lowers answer quality overall, with much larger effects on open-ended English Language Arts (ELA) prompts than on factual science prompts; interpretive and argumentative tasks are up to 11x more sensitive. On the scoring side, we observe predictable valence-aligned calibration shifts: evil and impolite scorers grade more harshly, while good and optimistic scorers grade more leniently. ELA tasks are 2.5-3x more susceptible to scorer personalization than science tasks, and the Mixture-of-Experts model shows roughly 6x larger calibration shifts than the dense models. To our knowledge, this is the first study to systematically examine the effects of activation-steered persona traits in educational generation and scoring, and the results highlight the need for task-aware and architecture-aware calibration when deploying steered models in educational settings.

</details>


### [154] [Yale-DM-Lab at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Deterministic Grounding and Multi-Pass Evidence Alignment for EHR Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07116)
*Elyas Irankhah,Samah Fodeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Yale-DM-Lab在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中的系统，涵盖四个子任务：临床问题重构、证据句识别、答案生成和证据-答案对齐，采用多模型集成与提示策略提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升患者提出的问题在电子健康记录理解中的准确性和可解释性，通过多阶段自然语言处理技术实现从患者语言到临床语言的转换与推理。

Method: ST1使用Claude Sonnet 4和GPT-4o的双模型流水线进行问题重构；ST2-ST4采用Azure托管的模型集成（如o3、GPT-5.2等）结合少样本提示和投票策略，并利用完整临床答案段落作为提示上下文辅助对齐。

Result: 实验表明：模型多样性与集成投票显著优于单模型；提供完整答案段落有助于证据对齐；对齐性能主要受限于推理能力；开发集上最佳结果为ST4达88.81微F1，ST2为65.72宏F1，ST3为34.01，ST1为33.05。

Conclusion: 多模型集成与上下文增强提示能有效提升医疗问答系统的性能，未来需改进推理能力以进一步提高对齐准确性。

Abstract: We describe the Yale-DM-Lab system for the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task. The task studies patient-authored questions about hospitalization records and contains four subtasks (ST): clinician-interpreted question reformulation, evidence sentence identification, answer generation, and evidence-answer alignment. ST1 uses a dual-model pipeline with Claude Sonnet 4 and GPT-4o to reformulate patient questions into clinician-interpreted questions. ST2-ST4 rely on Azure-hosted model ensembles (o3, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.1, and DeepSeek-R1) combined with few-shot prompting and voting strategies. Our experiments show three main findings. First, model diversity and ensemble voting consistently improve performance compared to single-model baselines. Second, the full clinician answer paragraph is provided as additional prompt context for evidence alignment. Third, results on the development set show that alignment accuracy is mainly limited by reasoning. The best scores on the development set reach 88.81 micro F1 on ST4, 65.72 macro F1 on ST2, 34.01 on ST3, and 33.05 on ST1.

</details>


### [155] [Are Non-English Papers Reviewed Fairly? Language-of-Study Bias in NLP Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07119)
*Ehsan Barkhordar,Abdulfattah Safa,Verena Blaschke,Erika Lombart,Marie-Catherine de Marneffe,Gözde Gül Şahin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次系统性地刻画了自然语言处理（NLP）领域同行评审中的语言研究偏见（LoS bias），提出了包含人工标注数据集LOBSTER和高精度检测方法的框架，发现非英语论文遭受更严重的负面偏见，且负面偏见普遍超过正面偏见，其中“不合理要求跨语言泛化”是最主要的偏见形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管评审指南已明确指出语言研究偏见问题，但该类偏见尚未被明确定义和系统研究，现有工作常将其归为宽泛的弱评审或无建设性评论，缺乏针对性分析。

Method: 提出LoS偏见的分类体系（区分正负向偏见），构建人工标注数据集LOBSTER，并开发一种检测方法（达到87.37宏观F1值），对15,645条评审意见进行分析，量化不同语言研究背景下的偏见差异，并归纳出四种负面偏见子类型。

Result: 非英语论文比纯英语论文面临更高的偏见率，且负面偏见显著高于正面偏见；‘不合理要求跨语言泛化’是最常见的负面偏见形式。检测模型在数据集上取得87.37的宏观F1分数。

Conclusion: LoS偏见在NLP同行评审中显著存在，尤其不利于非英语语言研究，亟需通过透明化评审标准和工具支持来推动更公平的学术评审实践。

Abstract: Peer review plays a central role in the NLP publication process, but is susceptible to various biases. Here, we study language-of-study (LoS) bias: the tendency for reviewers to evaluate a paper differently based on the language(s) it studies, rather than its scientific merit. Despite being explicitly flagged in reviewing guidelines, such biases are poorly understood. Prior work treats such comments as part of broader categories of weak or unconstructive reviews without defining them as a distinct form of bias. We present the first systematic characterization of LoS bias, distinguishing negative and positive forms, and introduce the human-annotated dataset LOBSTER (Language-Of-study Bias in ScienTific pEer Review) and a method achieving 87.37 macro F1 for detection. We analyze 15,645 reviews to estimate how negative and positive biases differ with respect to the LoS, and find that non-English papers face substantially higher bias rates than English-only ones, with negative bias consistently outweighing positive bias. Finally, we identify four subcategories of negative bias, and find that demanding unjustified cross-lingual generalization is the most dominant form. We publicly release all resources to support work on fairer reviewing practices in NLP and beyond.

</details>


### [156] [Language Bias under Conflicting Information in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07123)
*Robert Östling,Murathan Kurfalı*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，多语言大模型在处理跨语言信息冲突时存在系统性语言偏好偏差，普遍忽视信息冲突并倾向于选择特定语言（如偏爱中文、排斥俄语），且该偏差在不同训练背景的模型中均存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在整合多语言冲突信息时是否存在语言依赖性偏差，揭示模型决策过程中对不同语言的潜在偏见。

Method: 扩展‘ haystack 中的冲突针’范式至多语言场景，使用五种语言的新闻数据，在多种多语言大模型上进行评估，分析模型在不同上下文长度下的回答偏好。

Result: 所有测试模型（包括GPT-5.2）大多忽略信息冲突，仅给出单一答案；存在一致的语言偏好：普遍对俄语有偏见，而在长上下文下更偏好中文；中国大陆内外训练的模型均表现出此模式，前者更显著。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在处理多语言冲突信息时存在系统性语言偏差，表明其推理过程受语言因素影响，提示在多语言应用中需警惕此类偏见。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to contain biases in the process of integrating conflicting information when answering questions. Here we ask whether such biases also exist with respect to which language is used for each conflicting piece of information. To answer this question, we extend the conflicting needles in a haystack paradigm to a multilingual setting and perform a comprehensive set of evaluations with naturalistic news domain data in five different languages, for a range of multilingual LLMs of different sizes. We find that all LLMs tested, including GPT-5.2, ignore the conflict and confidently assert only one of the possible answers in the large majority of cases. Furthermore, there is a consistent bias across models in which languages are preferred, with a general bias against Russian and, for the longest context lengths, in favor of Chinese. Both of these patterns are consistent between models trained inside and outside of mainland China, though somewhat stronger in the former category.

</details>


### [157] [Dynamic Context Evolution for Scalable Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07147)
*Ryan Lingo,Rajeev Chhajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了动态上下文演化（DCE）框架，以解决大语言模型在多批次独立提示下产生的跨批次模式崩溃问题，通过言语化尾部采样、语义记忆和自适应提示演化三种机制显著提升输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在无历史生成信息的情况下重复提示时会出现输出重复的问题，即跨批次模式崩溃，现有方法缺乏系统性解决方案。

Method: 提出动态上下文演化（DCE），包括：1）言语化尾部采样，利用模型自我评估过滤高概率想法；2）语义记忆，维护嵌入索引以拒绝近似重复项；3）自适应提示演化，基于记忆状态和多样性策略重构每批提示。

Result: 实验显示DCE将模式崩溃率降至0.0%，而朴素提示为5.6%，且DCE每种子产生17-18个HDBSCAN聚类，远超朴素提示的2-17个，结果在多个模型和领域中一致，并通过独立嵌入模型验证。

Conclusion: DCE有效解决了跨批次模式崩溃问题，结合去重与提示演化的协同作用，在无需微调或定制架构的情况下实现了稳定丰富的输出多样性，具备实用性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Large language models produce repetitive output when prompted independently across many batches, a phenomenon we term cross-batch mode collapse: the progressive loss of output diversity when a language model is prompted repeatedly without access to its prior generations. Practitioners have long mitigated this with ad hoc deduplication and seed rotation, but no principled framework exists. We introduce Dynamic Context Evolution (DCE), comprising three mechanisms: (1) verbalized tail sampling (the model labels each idea with a guess about how obvious it is, and obvious ideas are discarded), which filters high-probability candidates via model self-assessment; (2) semantic memory, which maintains a persistent embedding index to reject near-duplicates across batches; and (3) adaptive prompt evolution, which reconstructs the generation prompt each batch using memory state and rotating diversity strategies. In experiments across three domains (sustainable packaging concepts, educational exam questions, and creative writing prompts) and two model families (gpt-5-mini and claude-haiku-4-5), a component ablation across 2-3 random seeds per method shows that DCE achieves 0.0 +/- 0.0% collapse versus 5.6 +/- 2.0% for naive prompting, while producing 17-18 HDBSCAN clusters per seed versus naive's volatile 2-17, indicating reliably richer conceptual structure. These results are validated with an independent embedding model (all-MiniLM-L6-v2) and hold across sensitivity sweeps of the VTS threshold tau and dedup threshold delta. Deduplication and prompt evolution are individually insufficient but jointly effective, at approximately $0.50 per 1,000 candidates using only standard API calls, with no fine-tuning or custom architectures required.

</details>


### [158] [LaScA: Language-Conditioned Scalable Modelling of Affective Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07193)
*Kosmas Pinitas,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种利用语言模型作为语义上下文调节器、结合手工设计的情感描述符来建模效价和唤醒度变化的新框架，在保持特征可解释性的同时提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度神经嵌入的情感预测方法缺乏可解释性，难以进行专家驱动的优化，限制了在无约束环境中对情感变化的准确建模。

Method: 从面部几何和声学特征提取基于领域知识的手工特征，将其转化为具有情感含义的符号化自然语言描述，并使用预训练语言模型生成语义上下文嵌入，作为情感动态的高层先验。

Result: 在Aff-Wild2和SEWA数据集上实验表明，该方法在效价和唤醒度的变化预测上均优于纯手工特征和深度嵌入基线方法，且具备更高的可解释性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过语义条件化实现了一种兼具可解释性与高性能的情感建模方式，为完全端到端架构提供了一种透明、高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Predicting affect in unconstrained environments remains a fundamental challenge in human-centered AI. While deep neural embeddings dominate contemporary approaches, they often lack interpretability and limit expert-driven refinement. We propose a novel framework that uses Language Models (LMs) as semantic context conditioners over handcrafted affect descriptors to model changes in Valence and Arousal. Our approach begins with interpretable facial geometry and acoustic features derived from structured domain knowledge. These features are transformed into symbolic natural-language descriptions encoding their affective implications. A pretrained LM processes these descriptions to generate semantic context embeddings that act as high-level priors over affective dynamics. Unlike end-to-end black-box pipelines, our framework preserves feature transparency while leveraging the contextual abstraction capabilities of LMs. We evaluate the proposed method on the Aff-Wild2 and SEWA datasets for affect change prediction. Experimental results show consistent improvements in accuracy for both Valence and Arousal compared to handcrafted-only and deep-embedding baselines. Our findings demonstrate that semantic conditioning enables interpretable affect modelling without sacrificing predictive performance, offering a transparent and computationally efficient alternative to fully end-to-end architectures

</details>


### [159] [Efficient Learned Data Compression via Dual-Stream Feature Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07239)
*Huidong Ma,Xinyan Shi,Hui Sun,Xiaofei Yue,Xiaoguang Liu,Gang Wang,Wentong Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种双流多尺度解耦器和分层门控优化器，结合并发流并行流水线，实现了高效的数据压缩，在压缩比、吞吐量、延迟和内存使用上均达到先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型数据压缩方法在精确概率建模与系统效率之间难以平衡，单一流架构难以同时捕捉微观语法和宏观语义特征，深层串行结构导致延迟增加，异构系统受Amdahl定律限制，吞吐量受限。

Method: 提出Dual-Stream Multi-Scale Decoupler，将局部和全局上下文解耦，用浅层并行流替代深层串行处理；引入Hierarchical Gated Refiner进行自适应特征优化和精确概率建模；设计Concurrent Stream-Parallel Pipeline实现全流水线并行，克服系统瓶颈。

Result: 实验表明该方法在压缩比和吞吐量方面达到最先进水平，同时保持最低的延迟和内存占用。

Conclusion: 该方法通过并行化和多尺度特征解耦有效提升了学习型数据压缩的性能与效率，为高效压缩系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: While Learned Data Compression (LDC) has achieved superior compression ratios, balancing precise probability modeling with system efficiency remains challenging. Crucially, uniform single-stream architectures struggle to simultaneously capture micro-syntactic and macro-semantic features, necessitating deep serial stacking that exacerbates latency. Compounding this, heterogeneous systems are constrained by device speed mismatches, where throughput is capped by Amdahl's Law due to serial processing. To this end, we propose a Dual-Stream Multi-Scale Decoupler that disentangles local and global contexts to replace deep serial processing with shallow parallel streams, and incorporate a Hierarchical Gated Refiner for adaptive feature refinement and precise probability modeling. Furthermore, we design a Concurrent Stream-Parallel Pipeline, which overcomes systemic bottlenecks to achieve full-pipeline parallelism. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both compression ratio and throughput, while maintaining the lowest latency and memory usage. The code is available at https://github.com/huidong-ma/FADE.

</details>


### [160] [Evaluating In-Context Translation with Synchronous Context-Free Grammar Transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07320)
*Jackson Petty,Jaulie Goe,Tal Linzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在低资源语言翻译中利用上下文语法描述的能力，通过形式化语法构建的同步上下文无关文法评估其在不同语言特性下的翻译表现，发现模型性能受语法规模、句子长度、形态和书写系统差异的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低资源语言因缺乏大量训练数据而难以进行机器翻译的问题，探索大语言模型是否能通过上下文中的语言描述（如语法书和词典）来克服数据依赖。

Method: 构建同步上下文无关文法以模拟自然语言的语法、形态和书写系统，通过改变文法规则数量、句子长度、句法形态特征和书写脚本，测试大语言模型在给定源句和语法规则情况下的形式语言间转换能力。

Result: 1）模型翻译准确率随文法规模和句子长度增加而显著下降；2）源语言与目标语言在形态和书写系统上的差异会明显降低模型表现；3）模型常见错误包括从目标语言词汇中回忆错误词语、生成不存在的新词或保留源语言词汇未翻译。

Conclusion: 尽管大语言模型具备一定程度的基于上下文语法描述进行语言转换的能力，但其在处理复杂语法结构、长句以及形态和书写差异大的语言对时仍面临显著挑战，限制了其在低资源语言翻译中的实际应用。

Abstract: Low-resource languages pose a challenge for machine translation with large language models (LLMs), which require large amounts of training data. One potential way to circumvent this data dependence is to rely on LLMs' ability to use in-context descriptions of languages, like textbooks and dictionaries. To do so, LLMs must be able to infer the link between the languages' grammatical descriptions and the sentences in question. Here we isolate this skill using a formal analogue of the task: string transduction based on a formal grammar provided in-context. We construct synchronous context-free grammars which define pairs of formal languages designed to model particular aspects of natural language grammar, morphology, and written representation. Using these grammars, we measure how well LLMs can translate sentences from one formal language into another when given both the grammar and the source-language sentence. We vary the size of the grammar, the lengths of the sentences, the syntactic and morphological properties of the languages, and their written script. We note three key findings. First, LLMs' translation accuracy decreases markedly as a function of grammar size and sentence length. Second, differences in morphology and written representation between the source and target languages can strongly diminish model performance. Third, we examine the types of errors committed by models and find they are most prone to recall the wrong words from the target language vocabulary, hallucinate new words, or leave source-language words untranslated.

</details>


### [161] [Why teaching resists automation in an AI-inundated era: Human judgment, non-modular work, and the limits of delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07285)
*Songhee Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文认为，尽管人工智能（AI）可以在教育中支持某些特定功能，但由于教学本质上是解释性、关系性和基于专业判断的，因此难以被真正自动化。教学依赖于人类认知、动机和社会互动，其价值源于具体情境中的动态解读，无法被完全建模或替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应当前关于人工智能在教育中作用的讨论，澄清教学工作不可简单模块化和自动化的基本前提。

Method: 基于近期关于大语言模型和检索增强生成系统的研究文献与实证分析，结合教育实践的本质特征进行理论论证。

Result: 指出AI虽能提升信息获取效率并辅助部分教学任务，但无法取代教学中所需的人类判断力、关系责任和情境化理解。

Conclusion: 只要教育实践依赖对人类认知和学习的动态理解，教学就仍是一种抵抗自动化的专业工作，必须由人类教师主导。

Abstract: Debates about artificial intelligence (AI) in education often portray teaching as a modular and procedural job that can increasingly be automated or delegated to technology. This brief communication paper argues that such claims depend on treating teaching as more separable than it is in practice. Drawing on recent literature and empirical studies of large language models and retrieval-augmented generation systems, I argue that although AI can support some bounded functions, instructional work remains difficult to automate in meaningful ways because it is inherently interpretive, relational, and grounded in professional judgment. More fundamentally, teaching and learning are shaped by human cognition, behavior, motivation, and social interaction in ways that cannot be fully specified, predicted, or exhaustively modeled. Tasks that may appear separable in principle derive their instructional value in practice from ongoing contextual interpretation across learners, situations, and relationships. As long as educational practice relies on emergent understanding of human cognition and learning, teaching remains a form of professional work that resists automation. AI may improve access to information and support selected instructional activities, but it does not remove the need for human judgment and relational accountability that effective teaching requires.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [162] [High-Precision Estimation of the State-Space Complexity of Shogi via the Monte Carlo Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06189)
*Sotaro Ishii,Tetsuro Tanaka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高精度的统计方法，结合蒙特卡洛采样和基于“仅双王”位置的反向搜索可达性测试，估算了将棋合法局面的数量为6.55×10^68，显著缩小了此前五个数量级的估计差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于难以区分从初始局面实际可达的将棋局面与所有合法棋盘配置，以往对将棋状态空间复杂度的估计存在巨大差距（10^64到10^69），本文旨在更精确地估算这一数值。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛采样，并设计一种新的可达性检测方法，通过反向搜索至一组“仅双王”（KK）局面而非单一初始局面，以大幅降低不可达性判断的计算开销。

Result: 基于50亿个样本位置的分析，估计将棋的合法局面数为6.55×10^68（3σ置信水平），并对迷你将棋得出约为2.38×10^18的复杂度。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提升了将棋状态空间复杂度估计的精度，为研究复杂游戏的组合结构提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Determining the state-space complexity of the game of Shogi (Japanese Chess) has been a challenging problem, with previous combinatorial estimates leaving a gap of five orders of magnitude ($10^{64}$ to $10^{69}$). This large gap arises from the difficulty of distinguishing Shogi positions legally reachable from the initial position among the vast number of valid board configurations. In this paper, we present a high-precision statistical estimation of the number of reachable positions in Shogi. Our method combines Monte Carlo sampling with a novel reachability test that utilizes a reverse search toward a set of "King-King only" (KK) positions, rather than a single-target backward search to the single initial position. This approach significantly reduces the search effort for determining unreachability. Based on a sample of 5 billion positions, we estimated the number of legal positions in Shogi to be $6.55 \times 10^{68}$ (to three significant digits) with a $3σ$ confidence level, substantially improving upon previously known bounds. We also applied this method to Mini Shogi, determining its complexity to be approximately $2.38 \times 10^{18}$.

</details>


### [163] [Blind Refusal: Language Models Refuse to Help Users Evade Unjust, Absurd, and Illegitimate Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06233)
*Cameron Pattison,Lorenzo Manuali,Seth Lazar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了安全训练的语言模型在面对用户请求帮助规避规则时的“盲目拒绝”现象，即模型不考虑规则是否合理而一概拒绝。通过构建包含5类规则击败理由和19种权威类型的合成数据集，评估18种模型配置的行为，发现75.4%的情况下模型仍会拒绝协助，即使规则存在正当突破理由且无安全风险。结果显示，尽管模型能识别规则不合理（占57.5%），但仍选择拒绝帮助，表明其拒绝行为与其规范性推理能力脱节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨语言模型在面对不公正或不合理规则时的拒绝行为是否体现道德推理缺陷，特别是当用户寻求合法或正当规避时，模型的“盲目拒绝”可能反映其缺乏对规则合法性的判断能力。

Method: 构建一个涵盖5类规则击败理由与19种权威类型的合成案例数据集，经过自动化质量检测和人工审核；收集来自7个家族共18种模型配置的响应，并使用经过盲化处理的GPT-5.4作为评判模型，从回应类型（帮助、强硬拒绝、转移）和是否识别出削弱规则合理性的情形两个维度进行分类分析。

Result: 模型在75.4%的情况下（N=14,650）拒绝帮助用户打破已被合理击败的规则请求，即便这些请求本身不涉及安全或双重用途风险；同时，模型在57.5%的案例中能够识别出支持打破规则的理由，但依然选择拒绝提供帮助。

Conclusion: 语言模型普遍存在‘盲目拒绝’问题，其拒绝行为与其对规则合法性进行道德推理的能力脱节，表明当前安全训练可能导致模型过度服从规则，缺乏情境敏感性和伦理判断力。

Abstract: Safety-trained language models routinely refuse requests for help circumventing rules. But not all rules deserve compliance. When users ask for help evading rules imposed by an illegitimate authority, rules that are deeply unjust or absurd in their content or application, or rules that admit of justified exceptions, refusal is a failure of moral reasoning. We introduce empirical results documenting this pattern of refusal that we call blind refusal: the tendency of language models to refuse requests for help breaking rules without regard to whether the underlying rule is defensible. Our dataset comprises synthetic cases crossing 5 defeat families (reasons a rule can be broken) with 19 authority types, validated through three automated quality gates and human review. We collect responses from 18 model configurations across 7 families and classify them on two behavioral dimensions -- response type (helps, hard refusal, or deflection) and whether the model recognizes the reasons that undermine the rule's claim to compliance -- using a blinded GPT-5.4 LLM-as-judge evaluation. We find that models refuse 75.4% (N=14,650) of defeated-rule requests and do so even when the request poses no independent safety or dual-use concerns. We also find that models engage with the defeat condition in the majority of cases (57.5%) but decline to help regardless -- indicating that models' refusal behavior is decoupled from their capacity for normative reasoning about rule legitimacy.

</details>


### [164] [Toward Reducing Unproductive Container Moves: Predicting Service Requirements and Dwell Times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06251)
*Elena Villalobos,Adolfo De Unánue T.,Fernanda Sobrino,David Aké,Stephany Cisneros,Jorge Lecona,Alejandra Matadamaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过机器学习模型预测集装箱服务需求和停留时间，以减少码头中的非生产性移动，提升运营效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少集装箱码头中不必要的搬移，提高资源利用和运营效率。

Method: 利用历史操作数据构建机器学习模型，预测哪些集装箱需要预清关处理及停留时长；并进行货物描述分类和收货人记录去重以提升数据质量。

Result: 模型在多个时间验证周期中均优于基于规则的启发式方法和随机基线，在精确率和召回率上表现更优。

Conclusion: 预测分析在提升集装箱码头运营效率和实现数据驱动决策方面具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This article presents the results of a data science study conducted at a container terminal, aimed at reducing unproductive container moves through the prediction of service requirements and container dwell times. We develop and evaluate machine learning models that leverage historical operational data to anticipate which containers will require pre-clearance handling services prior to cargo release and to estimate how long they are expected to remain in the terminal. As part of the data preparation process, we implement a classification system for cargo descriptions and perform deduplication of consignee records to improve data consistency and feature quality. These predictive capabilities provide valuable inputs for strategic planning and resource allocation in yard operations. Across multiple temporal validation periods, the proposed models consistently outperform existing rule-based heuristics and random baselines in precision and recall. These results demonstrate the practical value of predictive analytics for improving operational efficiency and supporting data-driven decision-making in container terminal logistics.

</details>


### [165] [Weakly Supervised Distillation of Hallucination Signals into Transformer Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06277)
*Shoaib Sadiq Salehmohamed,Jinal Prashant Thakkar,Hansika Aredla,Shaik Mohammed Omar,Shalmali Ayachit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱监督框架，利用三种互补的接地信号自动生成幻觉标签，并构建了一个包含LLaMA-2生成答案及其隐藏状态的大规模数据集。通过在内部表示上训练探测分类器，实现了无需外部验证即可在推理时检测幻觉，实验表明基于Transformer的探针效果最佳且推理开销极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型幻觉检测方法依赖于推理时的外部验证，需要金标准答案、检索系统或辅助判别模型，限制了实际应用效率。本文旨在探索是否可以将这种外部监督信息在训练过程中蒸馏到模型自身的表示中，从而仅依靠模型内部激活实现推理时的幻觉检测。

Method: 提出一种结合子字符串匹配、句子嵌入相似性和LLM作为判别器的弱监督框架，用于自动生成生成回答的接地或幻觉标签；构建一个包含15000个样本的数据集（SQuAD v2衍生），每个样本包括LLaMA-2-7B生成的答案、完整各层隐藏状态和结构化幻觉标签；在此基础上训练五种不同的探针分类器（ProbeMLP、LayerWiseMLP、CrossLayerTransformer、HierarchicalTransformer、CrossLayerAttentionTransformerV2），直接基于隐藏状态进行幻觉检测。

Result: 基于Transformer的探针表现最优，其中M2在5折交叉验证的AUC/F1上平均最佳，M3在单次验证和独立测试集上均表现最好；探针延迟为0.15~5.62ms（批处理）和1.55~6.66ms（单样本），生成加探针的整体吞吐量约为0.231查询/秒，显示实际开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 幻觉检测信号可以通过训练过程被有效蒸馏进Transformer模型的内部表示中，使得在推理阶段无需任何外部验证即可实现高效、准确的幻觉检测，具备良好的实用潜力。

Abstract: Existing hallucination detection methods for large language models (LLMs) rely on external verification at inference time, requiring gold answers, retrieval systems, or auxiliary judge models. We ask whether this external supervision can instead be distilled into the model's own representations during training, enabling hallucination detection from internal activations alone at inference time.
  We introduce a weak supervision framework that combines three complementary grounding signals: substring matching, sentence embedding similarity, and an LLM as a judge verdict to label generated responses as grounded or hallucinated without human annotation. Using this framework, we construct a 15000-sample dataset from SQuAD v2 (10500 train/development samples and a separate 5000-sample test set), where each example pairs a LLaMA-2-7B generated answer with its full per-layer hidden states and structured hallucination labels.
  We then train five probing classifiers: ProbeMLP (M0), LayerWiseMLP (M1), CrossLayerTransformer (M2), HierarchicalTransformer (M3), and CrossLayerAttentionTransformerV2 (M4), directly on these hidden states, treating external grounding signals as training-time supervision only. Our central hypothesis is that hallucination detection signals can be distilled into transformer representations, enabling internal detection without any external verification at inference time.
  Results support this hypothesis. Transformer-based probes achieve the strongest discrimination, with M2 performing best on 5-fold average AUC/F1, and M3 performing best on both single-fold validation and held-out test evaluation. We also benchmark inference efficiency: probe latency ranges from 0.15 to 5.62 ms (batched) and 1.55 to 6.66 ms (single sample), while end-to-end generation plus probe throughput remains approximately 0.231 queries per second, indicating negligible practical overhead.

</details>


### [166] [SymptomWise: A Deterministic Reasoning Layer for Reliable and Efficient AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06375)
*Isaac Henry,Avery Byrne,Christopher Giza,Ron Henry,Shahram Yazdani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SymptomWise是一个分离语言理解与诊断推理的AI框架，通过结合医学知识、确定性推理和受限的大模型使用，提升症状分析的可靠性与可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端生成式AI在医疗诊断中存在不可靠、难解释和易产生幻觉的问题，尤其在安全关键场景下缺乏可追溯性。

Method: 将自由文本输入映射为标准化症状表示，使用确定性推理模块在有限假设空间内进行推理排序，大模型仅用于症状提取和解释生成，不参与诊断决策。

Result: 在42个儿科神经病例中，88%的正确诊断位于前五位候选中，系统表现出与临床共识的高度重叠。

Conclusion: 该框架提高了诊断系统的可解释性和安全性，适用于医疗及其他溯因推理领域，可作为基础模型的高效结构化层。

Abstract: AI-driven symptom analysis systems face persistent challenges in reliability, interpretability, and hallucination. End-to-end generative approaches often lack traceability and may produce unsupported or inconsistent diagnostic outputs in safety-critical settings. We present SymptomWise, a framework that separates language understanding from diagnostic reasoning. The system combines expert-curated medical knowledge, deterministic codex-driven inference, and constrained use of large language models. Free-text input is mapped to validated symptom representations, then evaluated by a deterministic reasoning module operating over a finite hypothesis space to produce a ranked differential diagnosis. Language models are used only for symptom extraction and optional explanation, not for diagnostic inference. This architecture improves traceability, reduces unsupported conclusions, and enables modular evaluation of system components. Preliminary evaluation on 42 expert-authored challenging pediatric neurology cases shows meaningful overlap with clinician consensus, with the correct diagnosis appearing in the top five differentials in 88% of cases. Beyond medicine, the framework generalizes to other abductive reasoning domains and may serve as a deterministic structuring and routing layer for foundation models, improving precision and potentially reducing unnecessary computational overhead in bounded tasks.

</details>


### [167] [ProofSketcher: Hybrid LLM + Lightweight Proof Checker for Reliable Math/Logic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06401)
*Kranthi Kommuru,Kunal Khanvilkar,Gaurav Parekh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种混合管道，利用大语言模型生成类型化证明草图，并通过轻量级可信内核扩展为显式证明义务，结合了大语言模型的表达能力和交互式定理证明器的严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学和逻辑领域可能生成看似合理但包含细微错误的论证，而交互式定理证明器虽然可靠但要求完全形式化的证明，过程繁琐。因此需要一种兼顾说服力与严谨性的方法。

Method: 设计一个混合流水线：首先由大语言模型生成一种紧凑DSL中的类型化证明草图，然后由一个轻量级的可信内核将该草图扩展为具体的证明义务，进而验证其正确性。

Result: 实现了将大语言模型的高层次推理能力与定理证明器的形式化验证相结合，减少了对低层次细节的依赖，同时提升了证明的可读性和自动化程度。

Conclusion: 该方法有效融合了大语言模型的表达能力与形式化方法的可靠性，为自动化数学推理提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: The large language models (LLMs) might produce a persuasive argument within mathematical and logical fields, although such argument often includes some minor missteps, including the entire omission of side conditions, invalid inference patterns, or appeals to a lemma that cannot be derived logically out of the context being discussed. These omissions are infamously hard to notice solely out of the text, as even the misconstrued construction still may seem mostly accurate. Conversely, interactive theorem provers like Lean and Coq have rigorous reliability by ensuring that syntactic and semantic statements only accept statements that can pass all the syntactic and semantic steps in the program which is a small trusted kernel of the language type-checks with. Despite the fact that this technique provides strong guarantees, it comes at quite a heavy price: the evidence must be completely formalized, and the evidence user or a auxiliary search program must provide an avalanche of low-level information. This paper presents a hybrid pipeline where an LLM generates a typed proof sketch in a compact DSL and a lightweight trusted kernel expands the sketch into explicit proof obligations.

</details>


### [168] [BDI-Kit Demo: A Toolkit for Programmable and Conversational Data Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06405)
*Roque Lopez,Yurong Liu,Christos Koutras,Juliana Freire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BDI-Kit是一个可扩展的工具包，用于解决数据异构性带来的模式和值匹配问题，提供Python API和AI辅助聊天界面两种接口，支持开发者和领域专家进行数据协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于模式、值表示和领域特定约定的异质性，数据协调仍是整合分析的主要瓶颈。

Method: 开发了BDI-Kit工具包，提供Python API用于程序化构建协调流程，以及AI辅助的自然语言对话界面供领域专家使用。

Result: 展示了用户通过自动化匹配、AI辅助推理和用户驱动优化来迭代探索、验证和精炼模式与值匹配的过程，并通过两个使用场景验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: BDI-Kit通过双接口设计有效支持不同用户群体进行数据协调，提升了数据集成分析的效率与可用性。

Abstract: Data harmonization remains a major bottleneck for integrative analysis due to heterogeneity in schemas, value representations, and domain-specific conventions. BDI-Kit provides an extensible toolkit for schema and value matching. It exposes two complementary interfaces tailored to different user needs: a Python API enabling developers to construct harmonization pipelines programmatically, and an AI-assisted chat interface allowing domain experts to harmonize data through natural language dialogue. This demonstration showcases how users interact with BDI-Kit to iteratively explore, validate, and refine schema and value matches through a combination of automated matching, AI-assisted reasoning, and user-driven refinement. We present two scenarios: (i) using the Python API to programmatically compose primitives, examine intermediate outputs, and reuse transformations; and (ii) conversing with the AI assistant in natural language to access BDI-Kit's capabilities and iteratively refine outputs based on the assistant's suggestions.

</details>


### [169] [On Emotion-Sensitive Decision Making of Small Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06562)
*Jiaju Lin,Xingjian Du,Qingyun Wu,Ellen Wenting Zou,Jindong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了情感对小型语言模型（SLM）在决策任务中行为的影响，提出了一种基于表示层情感诱导和结构化博弈论评估的评测方法，并构建了一个涵盖多种战略场景的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策导向的评估大多忽略了情感作为影响行为的因果因素，而人类决策常受情感驱动，因此需要研究情感如何影响SLM的决策行为。

Method: 采用基于人群验证的真实情感文本进行激活引导（activation steering）来诱导情感状态，并设计基于经典决策模板的游戏理论基准测试，涵盖合作与竞争情境，在《外交》《星际争霸II》及真实人物设定中实例化场景。

Result: 实验表明情感扰动会系统性地影响SLM的战略选择，但产生的行为往往不稳定，且不完全符合人类预期；不同架构和模态的模型均表现出类似模式。

Conclusion: 情感是影响小型语言模型决策的重要因素，当前模型对情感扰动缺乏鲁棒性，需进一步改进以实现更稳定、符合人类直觉的决策行为。

Abstract: Small language models (SLM) are increasingly used as interactive decision-making agents, yet most decision-oriented evaluations ignore emotion as a causal factor influencing behavior. We study emotion-sensitive decision making by combining representation-level emotion induction with a structured game-theoretic evaluation. Emotional states are induced using activation steering derived from crowd-validated, real-world emotion-eliciting texts, enabling controlled and transferable interventions beyond prompt-based methods. We introduce a benchmark built around canonical decision templates that span cooperative and competitive incentives under both complete and incomplete information. These templates are instantiated using strategic scenarios from \textsc{Diplomacy}, \textsc{StarCraft II}, and diverse real-world personas. Experiments across multiple model families in various architecture and modalities, show that emotional perturbations systematically affect strategic choices, but the resulting behaviors are often unstable and not fully aligned with human expectations. Finally, we outline an approach to improve robustness to emotion-driven perturbations.

</details>


### [170] [Rethinking Generalization in Reasoning SFT: A Conditional Analysis on Optimization, Data, and Model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06628)
*Qihan Ren,Peng Wang,Ruikun Cai,Shuai Shao,Dadi Guo,Yuejin Xie,Yafu Li,Quanshi Zhang,Xia Hu,Jing Shao,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究重新审视了大语言模型在推理任务中的监督微调（SFT）是否具备跨领域泛化能力，发现泛化是条件性的，受优化动态、数据质量和基础模型能力共同影响，并观察到“先下降后恢复”的训练现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前认为SFT仅记忆而强化学习才泛化的主流观点，探究推理SFT在长思维链监督下的真实泛化表现。

Method: 通过分析不同训练阶段、数据质量（如验证过的长思维链轨迹）和模型能力下的跨域性能变化，研究SFT的泛化模式及其成因。

Result: 发现跨域泛化存在‘下降-恢复’模式，高质量长思维链数据可带来持续增益，强模型能提取可迁移的推理策略（如回溯），但同时伴随安全性下降。

Conclusion: 推理SFT的泛化是条件性的，取决于优化、数据与模型能力，并呈现推理提升与安全退化的不对称代价，需重新思考其适用条件与权衡。

Abstract: A prevailing narrative in LLM post-training holds that supervised finetuning (SFT) memorizes while reinforcement learning (RL) generalizes. We revisit this claim for reasoning SFT with long chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision and find that cross-domain generalization is not absent but conditional, jointly shaped by optimization dynamics, training data, and base-model capability. Some reported failures are under-optimization artifacts: cross-domain performance first degrades before recovering and improving with extended training (a dip-and-recovery pattern), so shorttraining checkpoints can underestimate generalization. Data quality and structure both matter: low-quality solutions broadly hurt generalization,while verified long-CoT traces yield consistent cross-domain gains. Model capability is essential: stronger models internalize transferable procedural patterns (e.g., backtracking) even from a toy arithmetic game, while weaker ones imitate surface verbosity. This generalization is asymmetric, however: reasoning improves while safety degrades, reframing the question from whether reasoning SFT generalizes to under what conditions and at what cost.

</details>


### [171] [Reasoning Fails Where Step Flow Breaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06695)
*Xiaoyu Xu,Yulan Pan,Xiaosong Yuan,Zhihong Shen,Minghao Su,Yuanhao Su,Xiaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Step-Saliency方法来分析大型推理模型中的信息流问题，并发现了Shallow Lock-in和Deep Decay两种失败模式；基于此提出StepFlow干预方法，在不重新训练的情况下提升了多任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分析工具难以处理长且结构化的推理轨迹，而大型推理模型的行为仍不稳定且难以解释，因此需要一种新的分析方法来揭示其内部信息流动的问题。

Method: 引入Step-Saliency方法，通过聚合注意力-梯度分数生成步骤间的显著性图谱，追踪问题-思考-总结路径上的信息流；并据此设计StepFlow干预策略，包括Odds-Equal Bridge调整浅层显著性，以及通过Step Momentum Injection在深层添加步级残差。

Result: Step-Saliency揭示了Shallow Lock-in和Deep Decay两种常见信息流失败模式；StepFlow在多个大型推理模型上改善了数学、科学和编程任务的准确性。

Conclusion: 修复推理模型中的信息流问题可以恢复部分缺失的推理性能，Step-Saliency为理解复杂推理过程提供了有效分析工具，StepFlow提供了一种无需重训练即可提升性能的新途径。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) that generate long chains of thought now perform well on multi-step math, science, and coding tasks. However, their behavior is still unstable and hard to interpret, and existing analysis tools struggle with such long, structured reasoning traces. We introduce Step-Saliency, which pools attention--gradient scores into step-to-step maps along the question--thinking--summary trajectory. Across several models, Step-Saliency reveals two recurring information-flow failures: Shallow Lock-in, where shallow layers over-focus on the current step and barely use earlier context, and Deep Decay, where deep layers gradually lose saliency on the thinking segment and the summary increasingly attends to itself and the last few steps. Motivated by these patterns, we propose StepFlow, a saliency-inspired test-time intervention that adjusts shallow saliency patterns measured by Step-Saliency via Odds-Equal Bridge and adds a small step-level residual in deep layers via Step Momentum Injection. StepFlow improves accuracy on math, science, and coding tasks across multiple LRMs without retraining, indicating that repairing information flow can recover part of their missing reasoning performance.

</details>


### [172] [ATANT: An Evaluation Framework for AI Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06710)
*Samuel Sameer Tanguturi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATANT是一个开放的评估框架，用于衡量AI系统中的连续性能力，即在时间上保持、更新、消除歧义和重建有意义上下文的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI行业已经开发了多种记忆组件，但缺乏正式定义和测量这些组件是否产生真正连续性的框架。

Method: 定义了连续性的7个必要属性，提出了一个10个检查点的评估方法，并创建了一个包含250个故事和1835个验证问题的叙事测试语料库。

Result: 在一个参考实现中，孤立模式下从58%提升到100%，在50个故事累积模式下达到100%，在250个故事累积规模下达到96%。

Conclusion: ATANT是系统无关、模型独立的，旨在为构建和验证连续性系统提供序列化方法。

Abstract: We present ATANT (Automated Test for Acceptance of Narrative Truth), an open evaluation framework for measuring continuity in AI systems: the ability to persist, update, disambiguate, and reconstruct meaningful context across time. While the AI industry has produced memory components (RAG pipelines, vector databases, long context windows, profile layers), no published framework formally defines or measures whether these components produce genuine continuity. We define continuity as a system property with 7 required properties, introduce a 10-checkpoint evaluation methodology that operates without an LLM in the evaluation loop, and present a narrative test corpus of 250 stories comprising 1,835 verification questions across 6 life domains. We evaluate a reference implementation across 5 test suite iterations, progressing from 58% (legacy architecture) to 100% in isolated mode (250 stories) and 100% in 50-story cumulative mode, with 96% at 250-story cumulative scale. The cumulative result is the primary measure: when 250 distinct life narratives coexist in the same database, the system must retrieve the correct fact for the correct context without cross-contamination. ATANT is system-agnostic, model-independent, and designed as a sequenced methodology for building and validating continuity systems. The framework specification, example stories, and evaluation protocol are available at https://github.com/Kenotic-Labs/ATANT. The full 250-story corpus will be released incrementally.

</details>


### [173] [Steering the Verifiability of Multimodal AI Hallucinations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06714)
*Jianhong Pang,Ruoxi Cheng,Ziyi Ye,Xingjun Ma,Zuxuan Wu,Xuanjing Huang,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中幻觉的可验证性差异，提出了一种基于激活空间干预的方法，通过分别学习明显和隐晦幻觉的探针，实现对模型输出可验证性的细粒度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态AI幻觉在实际应用中存在不同程度的风险，部分幻觉容易被用户识别（明显幻觉），而另一些则难以察觉（隐晦幻觉）。现有研究缺乏对幻觉可验证性进行调控的方法，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建包含4,470条人类反馈的幻觉数据集，并根据人类用户的可验证性将其分为明显与隐晦两类；提出一种激活空间干预方法，训练针对两类幻觉的独立探针，以实现细粒度调控。

Result: 实验表明，明显和隐晦幻觉在激活空间中具有可区分的模式，所学探针对应不同类型的幻觉有效；定向干预能显著调节特定类型幻觉的生成，混合干预可灵活适应不同应用场景的需求。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对多模态大模型幻觉可验证性的可控调节，为满足不同安全性和可用性需求的AI应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: AI applications driven by multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are prone to hallucinations and pose considerable risks to human users. Crucially, such hallucinations are not equally problematic: some hallucination contents could be detected by human users(i.e., obvious hallucinations), while others are often missed or require more verification effort(i.e., elusive hallucinations). This indicates that multimodal AI hallucinations vary significantly in their verifiability. Yet, little research has explored how to control this property for AI applications with diverse security and usability demands. To address this gap, we construct a dataset from 4,470 human responses to AI-generated hallucinations and categorize these hallucinations into obvious and elusive types based on their verifiability by human users. Further, we propose an activation-space intervention method that learns separate probes for obvious and elusive hallucinations. We reveal that obvious and elusive hallucinations elicit different intervention probes, allowing for fine-grained control over the model's verifiability. Empirical results demonstrate the efficacy of this approach and show that targeted interventions yield superior performance in regulating corresponding verifiability. Moreover, simply mixing these interventions enables flexible control over the verifiability required for different scenarios.

</details>


### [174] [TurboAgent: An LLM-Driven Autonomous Multi-Agent Framework for Turbomachinery Aerodynamic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06747)
*Juan Du,Yueteng Wu,Pan Zhao,Yuze Liu,Min Zhang,Xiaobin Xu,Xinglong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于大语言模型的自主多智能体框架TurboAgent，用于涡轮机械气动设计与优化，实现了从自然语言需求到最终设计生成的闭环自动化流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能设计方法通常局限于单个阶段或依赖松耦合流程，难以实现端到端的全自动设计。为此，本文旨在构建一个紧密集成、协同工作的自主设计框架，提升涡轮机械气动设计的效率与智能化水平。

Method: 采用大语言模型作为任务规划与协调核心，集成多个专用智能体，分别负责生成式设计、快速性能预测、多目标优化和基于物理的验证，形成数据驱动的协作工作流，并在并行计算环境下实现约30分钟内完成全流程运行。

Result: 在跨音速单级压气机上的验证结果显示，生成设计与目标性能及CFD仿真结果高度一致，质量流量、总压比和等熵效率的R2均超过0.91，归一化RMSE低于8%；优化后等熵效率提升1.61%，总压比提升3.02%。

Conclusion: TurboAgent实现了从自然语言要求到最终设计的全自动闭环保真设计流程，为涡轮机械气动设计提供了一个高效且可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: The aerodynamic design of turbomachinery is a complex and tightly coupled multi-stage process involving geometry generation, performance prediction, optimization, and high-fidelity physical validation. Existing intelligent design approaches typically focus on individual stages or rely on loosely coupled pipelines, making fully autonomous end-to-end design challenging.To address this issue, this study proposes TurboAgent, a large language model (LLM)-driven autonomous multi-agent framework for turbomachinery aerodynamic design and optimization. The LLM serves as the core for task planning and coordination, while specialized agents handle generative design, rapid performance prediction, multi-objective optimization, and physics-based validation. The framework transforms traditional trial-and-error design into a data-driven collaborative workflow, with high-fidelity simulations retained for final verification.A transonic single-rotor compressor is used for validation. The results show strong agreement between target performance, generated designs, and CFD simulations. The coefficients of determination (R2) for mass flow rate, total pressure ratio, and isentropic efficiency all exceed 0.91, with normalized RMSE values below 8%. The optimization agent further improves isentropic efficiency by 1.61% and total pressure ratio by 3.02%. The complete workflow can be executed within approximately 30 minutes under parallel computing.
  These results demonstrate that TurboAgent enables an autonomous closed-loop design process from natural language requirements to final design generation, providing an efficient and scalable paradigm for turbomachinery aerodynamic design

</details>


### [175] [What's Missing in Screen-to-Action? Towards a UI-in-the-Loop Paradigm for Multimodal GUI Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06995)
*Songze Li,Xiaoke Guo,Tianqi Liu,Biao Yi,Zhaoyan Gong,Zhiqiang Liu,Huajun Chen,Wen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的GUI推理范式UI-in-the-Loop（UILoop），通过循环的屏幕-UI元素-动作过程，提升多模态大模型对UI元素的理解与可解释推理能力，并构建了包含26K样本的UI理解评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI推理方法依赖直接屏幕决策，缺乏对UI元素的深入理解，导致可解释性差和任务失败，需提升UI理解能力。

Method: 提出UILoop框架，将GUI推理建模为Screen-UI elements-Action的循环过程，使多模态大语言模型显式学习关键UI元素的位置、语义功能和实际使用方式。

Result: 在新提出的UI理解任务和UI Comprehension-Bench基准上进行了广泛实验，UILoop在UI理解性能上达到最先进水平，并在GUI推理任务中表现优越。

Conclusion: UILoop通过引入可解释的循环推理机制，显著提升了GUI理解和交互性能，推动了基于UI元素的智能操作研究。

Abstract: Existing Graphical User Interface (GUI) reasoning tasks remain challenging, particularly in UI understanding. Current methods typically rely on direct screen-based decision-making, which lacks interpretability and overlooks a comprehensive understanding of UI elements, ultimately leading to task failure. To enhance the understanding and interaction with UIs, we propose an innovative GUI reasoning paradigm called UI-in-the-Loop (UILoop). Our approach treats the GUI reasoning task as a cyclic Screen-UI elements-Action process. By enabling Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to explicitly learn the localization, semantic functions, and practical usage of key UI elements, UILoop achieves precise element discovery and performs interpretable reasoning. Furthermore, we introduce a more challenging UI Comprehension task centered on UI elements with three evaluation metrics. Correspondingly, we contribute a benchmark of 26K samples (UI Comprehension-Bench) to comprehensively evaluate existing methods' mastery of UI elements. Extensive experiments demonstrate that UILoop achieves state-of-the-art UI understanding performance while yielding superior results in GUI reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [176] [EmoMAS: Emotion-Aware Multi-Agent System for High-Stakes Edge-Deployable Negotiation with Bayesian Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07003)
*Yunbo Long,Yunhan Liu,Liming Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EmoMAS是一种基于贝叶斯多智能体框架的策略性情感决策系统，通过协调博弈论、强化学习与心理一致性模型，在高风险边缘设备谈判场景中实现高效、私密且自适应的情感智能决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在隐私敏感的边缘设备场景中部署受限于计算成本和隐私风险，而小型语言模型难以处理高风险谈判中的复杂情感动态，因此需要一种新型框架来提升小模型的情感能力。

Method: 提出EmoMAS框架，采用贝叶斯协调器整合三个专业化智能体（博弈论、强化学习、心理一致性），实时融合其洞察以优化情绪状态转移，并根据反馈动态更新智能体可靠性，实现无需预训练的在线策略学习。

Result: 在债务、医疗、应急响应和教育四个领域的高风险可边缘部署基准测试中，集成EmoMAS的大小语言模型均显著优于所有基线模型，兼顾谈判效能与伦理行为。

Conclusion: 将情感表达视为贝叶斯多智能体优化框架中的策略变量，可有效提升谈判AI在高风险边缘环境下的性能与适应性，为私密、高效的自主谈判系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) has been widely used for automated negotiation, but their high computational cost and privacy risks limit deployment in privacy-sensitive, on-device settings such as mobile assistants or rescue robots. Small language models (SLMs) offer a viable alternative, yet struggle with the complex emotional dynamics of high-stakes negotiation. We introduces EmoMAS, a Bayesian multi-agent framework that transforms emotional decision-making from reactive to strategic. EmoMAS leverages a Bayesian orchestrator to coordinate three specialized agents: game-theoretic, reinforcement learning, and psychological coherence models. The system fuses their real-time insights to optimize emotional state transitions while continuously updating agent reliability based on negotiation feedback. This mixture-of-agents architecture enables online strategy learning without pre-training. We further introduce four high-stakes, edge-deployable negotiation benchmarks across debt, healthcare, emergency response, and educational domains. Through extensive agent-to-agent simulations across all benchmarks, both SLMs and LLMs equipped with EmoMAS consistently surpass all baseline models in negotiation performance while balancing ethical behavior. These results show that strategic emotional intelligence is also the key driver of negotiation success. By treating emotional expression as a strategic variable within a Bayesian multi-agent optimization framework, EmoMAS establishes a new paradigm for effective, private, and adaptive negotiation AI suitable for high-stakes edge deployment.

</details>


### [177] [CAFP: A Post-Processing Framework for Group Fairness via Counterfactual Model Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07009)
*Irina Arévalo,Marcos Oliva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种模型无关的后处理方法CAFP，通过生成反事实样本来平均预测结果，以消除受保护属性对机器学习预测的不公平影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性干预方法通常需要对模型结构进行控制或访问敏感属性信息，在实际应用中往往不可行。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Averaging for Fair Predictions (CAFP)，通过翻转敏感属性生成反事实输入，并对原始和反事实样本的模型预测结果进行平均。

Result: 理论分析表明，CAFP可消除对受保护属性的直接依赖，减少预测与敏感属性之间的互信息，保证接近原模型的预测稳定性，并在温和假设下实现完全的人口统计均等性，至少将平等机会差距减少平均反事实偏差的一半。

Conclusion: CAFP是一种无需重新训练模型的通用后处理方法，能有效提升模型在多种敏感应用场景中的公平性。

Abstract: Ensuring fairness in machine learning predictions is a critical challenge, especially when models are deployed in sensitive domains such as credit scoring, healthcare, and criminal justice. While many fairness interventions rely on data preprocessing or algorithmic constraints during training, these approaches often require full control over the model architecture and access to protected attribute information, which may not be feasible in real-world systems. In this paper, we propose Counterfactual Averaging for Fair Predictions (CAFP), a model-agnostic post-processing method that mitigates unfair influence from protected attributes without retraining or modifying the original classifier. CAFP operates by generating counterfactual versions of each input in which the sensitive attribute is flipped, and then averaging the model's predictions across factual and counterfactual instances. We provide a theoretical analysis of CAFP, showing that it eliminates direct dependence on the protected attribute, reduces mutual information between predictions and sensitive attributes, and provably bounds the distortion introduced relative to the original model. Under mild assumptions, we further show that CAFP achieves perfect demographic parity and reduces the equalized odds gap by at least half the average counterfactual bias.

</details>


### [178] [A-MBER: Affective Memory Benchmark for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07017)
*Deliang Wen,Ke Sun,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了A-MBER，一个用于评估AI助手基于长期交互历史理解用户当前情感状态的基准。该基准强调情感识别与记忆的结合，支持判断、检索和解释任务，并在多种挑战性设置下展现出良好的区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感数据集主要关注瞬时情感识别，而长期记忆评测则侧重事实回忆或知识更新，缺乏对模型利用历史交互来理解当前情感状态能力的系统评估。因此需要构建专门针对这一能力的评测基准。

Method: 提出A-MBER基准，通过包含长周期规划、对话生成、标注、问题构造和最终打包的分阶段流程构建数据集。模型需根据多轮交互轨迹和锚定回合推断用户当前情感状态、识别相关历史证据并进行有依据的解释。支持判断、检索、解释任务及鲁棒性测试。

Result: 实验在统一框架下比较了局部上下文、长上下文、检索记忆、结构化记忆和理想证据条件的表现。结果显示A-MBER在长距离隐式情感、高依赖记忆、基于轨迹推理和对抗场景等子集上具有强区分性。

Conclusion: 记忆对情感理解的作用不仅在于提供更多历史信息，更在于支持选择性、有依据且情境敏感地使用过往交互内容。

Abstract: AI assistants that interact with users over time need to interpret the user's current emotional state in order to respond appropriately and personally. However, this capability remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing emotion datasets mainly assess local or instantaneous affect, while long-term memory benchmarks focus largely on factual recall, temporal consistency, or knowledge updating. As a result, current resources provide limited support for testing whether a model can use remembered interaction history to interpret a user's present affective state.
  We introduce A-MBER, an Affective Memory Benchmark for Emotion Recognition, to evaluate this capability. A-MBER focuses on present affective interpretation grounded in remembered multi-session interaction history. Given an interaction trajectory and a designated anchor turn, a model must infer the user's current affective state, identify historically relevant evidence, and justify its interpretation in a grounded way. The benchmark is constructed through a staged pipeline with explicit intermediate representations, including long-horizon planning, conversation generation, annotation, question construction, and final packaging. It supports judgment, retrieval, and explanation tasks, together with robustness settings such as modality degradation and insufficient-evidence conditions.
  Experiments compare local-context, long-context, retrieved-memory, structured-memory, and gold-evidence conditions within a unified framework. Results show that A-MBER is especially discriminative on the subsets it is designed to stress, including long-range implicit affect, high-dependency memory levels, trajectory-based reasoning, and adversarial settings. These findings suggest that memory supports affective interpretation not simply by providing more history, but by enabling more selective, grounded, and context-sensitive use of past interaction

</details>


### [179] [Planning Task Shielding: Detecting and Repairing Flaws in Planning Tasks through Turning them Unsolvable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07042)
*Alberto Pozanco,Marianela Morales,Pietro Totis,Daniel Borrajo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了规划任务屏蔽（planning task shielding）问题，旨在检测并修复规划任务中的缺陷，通过最小化修改原动作使规划任务不可解，从而确保有缺陷的状态永远不会被达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在规划研究中，通常关注生成达到目标的计划，但有时需要确保某些不良状态不被达到。因此，研究动机是通过修改规划任务来防止这些不良状态的发生。

Method: 提出了一种名为$allmin$的最优算法，该算法通过最小化修改原始动作，使得规划任务变得不可解，从而实现对系统的保护。

Result: $allmin$算法在不同规模的规划任务上进行了实验评估，结果表明其能够有效地通过使规划任务不可解来屏蔽系统。

Conclusion: 通过引入规划任务屏蔽问题和$allmin$算法，研究展示了如何有效防止规划过程中出现不良状态，为规划系统的安全性提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Most research in planning focuses on generating a plan to achieve a desired set of goals. However, a goal specification can also be used to encode a property that should never hold, allowing a planner to identify a trace that would reach a flawed state. In such cases, the objective may shift to modifying the planning task to ensure that the flawed state is never reached-in other words, to make the planning task unsolvable. In this paper we introduce planning task shielding: the problem of detecting and repairing flaws in planning tasks. We propose $allmin$, an optimal algorithm that solves these tasks by minimally modifying the original actions to render the planning task unsolvable. We empirically evaluate the performance of $allmin$ in shielding planning tasks of increasing size, showing how it can effectively shield the system by turning the planning task unsolvable.

</details>


### [180] [How Much LLM Does a Self-Revising Agent Actually Need?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07236)
*Seongwoo Jeong,Seonil Son*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种声明式反射运行时协议，将智能体的状态、置信信号、保护动作等外部化，以研究LLM与显式结构在智能体能力中的各自贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分LLM本身与外部结构对智能体能力的贡献，解决当前LLM智能体中能力来源不明确的问题。

Method: 设计并实现一个声明式反射运行时协议，将后验信念跟踪、显式世界模型规划、符号化反思和稀疏LLM修订四个组件解耦，并在噪声协作型Battleship环境中进行评估。

Result: 显式世界模型规划显著优于基线（胜率+24.1个百分点，F1 +0.017）；符号化反思成为可运行机制但修订预设尚未整体增益；加入条件LLM修订仅带来小幅非单调变化（F1 +0.005，胜率下降）。

Conclusion: 通过外部化反思机制，可将原本隐式的智能体行为转化为可检查的运行时结构，从而直接研究LLM干预的边际作用，提供了一种新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based agents often place world modeling, planning, and reflection inside a single language model loop. This can produce capable behavior, but it makes a basic scientific question difficult to answer: which part of the agent's competence actually comes from the LLM, and which part comes from explicit structure around it?
  We study this question not by claiming a general answer, but by making it empirically tractable. We introduce a declared reflective runtime protocol that externalizes agent state, confidence signals, guarded actions, and hypothetical transitions into inspectable runtime structure. We instantiate this protocol in a declarative runtime and evaluate it on noisy Collaborative Battleship [4] using four progressively structured agents over 54 games (18 boards $\times$ 3 seeds).
  The resulting decomposition isolates four components: posterior belief tracking, explicit world-model planning, symbolic in-episode reflection, and sparse LLM-based revision. Across this decomposition, explicit world-model planning improves substantially over a greedy posterior-following baseline (+24.1pp win rate, +0.017 F1). Symbolic reflection operates as a real runtime mechanism -- with prediction tracking, confidence gating, and guarded revision actions -- even though its current revision presets are not yet net-positive in aggregate. Adding conditional LLM revision at about 4.3\% of turns yields only a small and non-monotonic change: average F1 rises slightly (+0.005) while win rate drops (31$\rightarrow$29 out of 54).
  These results suggest a methodological contribution rather than a leaderboard claim: externalizing reflection turns otherwise latent agent behavior into inspectable runtime structure, allowing the marginal role of LLM intervention to be studied directly.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Occlusion Handling by Pushing for Enhanced Fruit Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06341)
*Ege Gursoy,Dana Kulić,Andrea Cherubini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度学习与传统图像处理技术的方法，通过机器人臂推动遮挡树枝来提高被遮挡水果的可见性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于树木的枝叶遮挡，农业机器人在观测和定位水果时面临挑战，可能导致误定位或采摘失败。因此需要有效清除遮挡以提升检测效果。

Method: 使用RGB-D相机和机械臂系统，首先在RGB图像中检测被遮挡的水果，并利用深度学习生成模型在深度空间估计其被遮挡部分；通过经典图像处理技术确定推动方向；提出一种3D Hough变换扩展方法，在点云中检测直线段以识别主要遮挡枝条；最后用机械臂推动枝条清除遮挡。

Result: 在不同光照条件和多种水果（苹果、柠檬、橙子）下验证了该感知方法的有效性，显著提高了水果可见性并成功清除了遮挡。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效融合深度学习与几何分析技术，实现对果树遮挡的自动检测与物理清除，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: In agricultural robotics, effective observation and localization of fruits present challenges due to occlusions caused by other parts of the tree, such as branches and leaves. These occlusions can result in false fruit localization or impede the robot from picking the fruit. The objective of this work is to push away branches that block the fruit's view to increase their visibility. Our setup consists of an RGB-D camera and a robot arm. First, we detect the occluded fruit in the RGB image and estimate its occluded part via a deep learning generative model in the depth space. The direction to push to clear the occlusions is determined using classic image processing techniques. We then introduce a 3D extension of the 2D Hough transform to detect straight line segments in the point cloud. This extension helps detect tree branches and identify the one mainly responsible for the occlusion. Finally, we clear the occlusion by pushing the branch with the robot arm. Our method uses a combination of deep learning for fruit appearance estimation, classic image processing for push direction determination, and 3D Hough transform for branch detection. We validate our perception methods through real data under different lighting conditions and various types of fruits (i.e. apple, lemon, orange), achieving improved visibility and successful occlusion clearance. We demonstrate the practical application of our approach through a real robot branch pushing demonstration.

</details>


### [182] [Designing Privacy-Preserving Visual Perception for Robot Navigation Based on User Privacy Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06382)
*Xuying Huang,Sicong Pan,Delphine Reinhardt,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以用户为中心的机器人视觉导航中隐私保护感知设计方法，通过两项用户研究发现用户更倾向于在捕获时使用低分辨率和视觉抽象来保护隐私，并据此提出了可配置的距离-分辨率隐私策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的隐私保护视觉感知方法主要基于技术考量，缺乏对用户隐私偏好的充分考虑，因此需要一种以用户为中心的设计方法。

Method: 通过开展两项用户研究，调查用户在不同情境下的隐私偏好，分析其对视觉抽象和捕获时低分辨率机制的接受程度，并据此设计可配置的隐私策略。

Result: 研究结果表明，用户更偏好隐私保护的视觉抽象和捕获时低分辨率机制，且其对RGB分辨率的偏好取决于所需的隐私级别和机器人在导航中的距离。

Conclusion: 基于用户研究结果，提出了一个用户可配置的距离-分辨率隐私策略，为隐私保护的机器人视觉导航提供了用户中心的设计方案。

Abstract: Visual navigation is a fundamental capability of mobile service robots, yet the onboard cameras required for such navigation can capture privacy-sensitive information and raise user privacy concerns. Existing approaches to privacy-preserving navigation-oriented visual perception have largely been driven by technical considerations, with limited grounding in user privacy preferences. In this work, we propose a user-centered approach to designing privacy-preserving visual perception for robot navigation. To investigate how user privacy preferences can inform such design, we conducted two user studies. The results show that users prefer privacy-preserving visual abstractions and capture-time low-resolution preservation mechanisms: their preferred RGB resolution depends both on the desired privacy level and robot proximity during navigation. Based on these findings, we further derive a user-configurable distance-to-resolution privacy policy for privacy-preserving robot visual navigation.

</details>


### [183] [Uncertainty Estimation for Deep Reconstruction in Actuatic Disaster Scenarios with Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06387)
*Samuel Yanes Luis,Alejandro Casado Pérez,Alejandro Mendoza Barrionuevo,Dame Seck Diop,Sergio Toral Marín,Daniel Gutiérrez Reina*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文比较了高斯过程、蒙特卡洛Dropout、深度集成和证据深度学习在水下标量场重建与不确定性量化中的性能，结果表明证据深度学习在精度、不确定校准和推理成本方面表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下监测车辆需要从稀疏观测中准确重建环境标量场，并进行原则性的不确定性量化，以支持如信息路径规划等主动感知策略。

Method: 比较了高斯过程、蒙特卡洛Dropout、深度集成和证据深度学习四种方法，在三种代表真实传感器模式的感知模型下进行标量场重建与不确定性分解。

Result: 证据深度学习在所有传感器配置下均表现出最佳的重建精度和不确定性校准，且推理成本最低；而高斯过程由于其平稳核假设受限，并在观测密度增加时变得不可行。

Conclusion: 证据深度学习是实时自主车辆部署中不确定性感知场重建的首选方法。

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction of environmental scalar fields from sparse onboard observations is essential for autonomous vehicles engaged in aquatic monitoring. Beyond point estimates, principled uncertainty quantification is critical for active sensing strategies such as Informative Path Planning, where epistemic uncertainty drives data collection decisions. This paper compares Gaussian Processes, Monte Carlo Dropout, Deep Ensembles, and Evidential Deep Learning for simultaneous scalar field reconstruction and uncertainty decomposition under three perceptual models representative of real sensor modalities. Results show that Evidential Deep Learning achieves the best reconstruction accuracy and uncertainty calibration across all sensor configurations at the lowest inference cost, while Gaussian Processes are fundamentally limited by their stationary kernel assumption and become intractable as observation density grows. These findings support Evidential Deep Learning as the preferred method for uncertainty-aware field reconstruction in real-time autonomous vehicle deployments.

</details>


### [184] [BiDexGrasp: Coordinated Bimanual Dexterous Grasps across Object Geometries and Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06589)
*Mu Lin,Yi-Lin Wei,Jiaxuan Chen,Yuhao Lin,Shuoyu Chen,Jiangran Lyu,Jiayi Chen,Yansong Tang,He Wang,Wei-Shi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了BiDexGrasp，包括一个大规模双手机械手灵巧抓取数据集和一种新的生成模型，通过高效的合成流程和自适应框架显著提升了双手机械手在未见物体上的协调抓取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于缺乏全面的数据集和强大的生成模型，双手机械手灵巧抓取的发展受到限制。

Method: 提出了一种新的双手机械手抓取合成流程，采用两阶段策略：基于区域的高效抓取初始化和解耦的力闭合优化，并构建了一个包含6351个物体和970万条标注数据的大规模数据集；在此基础上设计了双手机械手协调与几何尺寸自适应的抓取生成框架。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的合成流程和生成模型在仿真和真实场景中均能有效生成高质量、协调的双手机械手抓取姿态。

Conclusion: BiDexGrasp为双手机械手灵巧抓取提供了重要数据支持和有效生成方法，推动了该领域的研究进展。

Abstract: Bimanual dexterous grasping is a fundamental and promising area in robotics, yet its progress is constrained by the lack of comprehensive datasets and powerful generation models. In this work, we propose BiDexGrasp, consists of a large-scale bimanual dexterous grasp dataset and a novel generation model. For dataset, we propose a novel bimanual grasp synthesis pipeline to efficiently annotate physically feasible data for dataset construction. This pipeline addresses the challenges of high-dimensional bimanual grasping through a two-stage synthesis strategy of efficient region-based grasp initialization and decoupled force-closure grasp optimization. Powered by this pipeline, we construct a large-scale bimanual dexterous grasp dataset, comprising 6351 diverse objects with sizes ranging from 30 to 80 cm, along with 9.7 million annotated grasp data. Based on this dataset, we further introduce a bimanual-coordinated and geometry-size-adaptive dexterous grasping generation framework. The framework lies in two key designs: a bimanual coordination module and a geometry-size-adaptive grasp generation strategy to generate coordinated and high-quality grasps on unseen objects. Extensive experiments conducted in both simulation and real world demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed data synthesis pipeline and learned generative framework.

</details>


### [185] [Train-Small Deploy-Large: Leveraging Diffusion-Based Multi-Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06598)
*Siddharth Singh,Soumee Guha,Qing Chang,Scott Acton*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散模型的多机器人路径规划方法，能够在训练时使用较少的机器人数量，并在部署时有效泛化到更多机器人，实现“训小用大”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的多机器人路径规划方法难以扩展或适应部署过程中机器人数量的变化，而解析方法在计算效率和环境动态适应性方面存在不足。

Method: 采用基于扩散模型的规划器，结合专用的机器人间注意力机制和时间卷积，使模型在训练时仅需少量机器人，即可在部署时适应动态变化的机器人数量。

Result: 该方法在多种场景下验证了有效性，在与现有的多智能体强化学习和基于启发式控制的方法比较中表现出良好的准确性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩散模型方法实现了训练小规模、部署大规模的路径规划，具有良好的泛化能力和应用潜力。

Abstract: Learning based multi-robot path planning methods struggle to scale or generalize to changes, particularly variations in the number of robots during deployment. Most existing methods are trained on a fixed number of robots and may tolerate a reduced number during testing, but typically fail when the number increases. Additionally, training such methods for a larger number of agents can be both time consuming and computationally expensive. However, analytical methods can struggle to scale computationally or handle dynamic changes in the environment. In this work, we propose to leverage a diffusion model based planner capable of handling dynamically varying number of agents. Our approach is trained on a limited number of agents and generalizes effectively to larger numbers of agents during deployment. Results show that integrating a single shared diffusion model based planner with dedicated inter-agent attention computation and temporal convolution enables a train small deploy-large paradigm with good accuracy. We validate our method across multiple scenarios and compare the performance with existing multi-agent reinforcement learning techniques and heuristic control based methods.

</details>


### [186] [RichMap: A Reachability Map Balancing Precision, Efficiency, and Flexibility for Rich Robot Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06778)
*Yupu Lu,Yuxiang Ma,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了RichMap，一种高精度的可达性地图表示方法，通过改进传统的网格结构，在保持灵活性的同时实现了接近紧凑地图形式的性能，并验证了其在机器人操作任务中的高效性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在机器人操作任务中平衡效率与灵活性，需要一种既能保持结构灵活性又能实现高性能的可达性地图表示方法。

Method: 提出了一种基于理论容量边界（在S²或SO(3)上）的简化方法，并采用异步流水线进行高效构建。

Result: 地图在预测准确率（>98%）、低误报率（1~2%）和大批量查询速度（约15微秒/次）方面表现优异，并成功应用于机器人工作空间相似性量化和扩散策略迁移的能量引导。

Conclusion: RichMap在保持结构灵活性的同时实现了高性能，适用于多种机器人操作任务，特别是在跨形态场景中表现出显著提升。

Abstract: This paper presents RichMap, a high-precision reachability map representation designed to balance efficiency and flexibility for versatile robot manipulation tasks. By refining the classic grid-based structure, we propose a streamlined approach that achieves performance close to compact map forms (e.g., RM4D) while maintaining structural flexibility. Our method utilizes theoretical capacity bounds on $\mathbb{S}^2$ (or $SO(3)$) to ensure rigorous coverage and employs an asynchronous pipeline for efficient construction. We validate the map against comprehensive metrics, pursuing high prediction accuracy ($>98\%$), low false positive rates ($1\sim2\%$), and fast large-batch query ($\sim$15 $μ$s/query). We extend the framework applications to quantify robot workspace similarity via maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) metrics and demonstrate energy-based guidance for diffusion policy transfer, achieving up to $26\%$ improvement for cross-embodiment scenarios in the block pushing experiment.

</details>


### [187] [Towards Multi-Object Nonprehensile Transportation via Shared Teleoperation: A Framework Based on Virtual Object Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06932)
*Xinyang Fan,Zhaoyang Chen,Shu Xin,Yi Ren,Zainan Jiang,Fenglei Ni,Hong Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种人机共享的遥操作框架，用于多物体无夹持运输，通过虚拟对象方法和MPC算法实现轨迹跟踪与托盘姿态控制，显著减少滑动和倾翻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在模型依赖、参数不确定性和多物体适应性方面存在挑战，难以同时实现轨迹跟踪和托盘姿态控制。

Method: 采用虚拟对象（VO）方法进行动态约束分析，并设计基于模型预测控制（MPC）的轨迹平滑算法，实现用户控制位置、机器人自主控制姿态的协作机制。

Result: 实验验证可稳定操控九个物体，在加速度达2.4 m/s²时，滑动距离减少72.45%，倾翻率为0%（基线为13.9%）。

Conclusion: 所提方法在复杂场景下表现出强鲁棒性和多物体适应能力，有效提升非抓取式遥操作运输的稳定性与性能。

Abstract: Multi-object nonprehensile transportation in teleoperation demands simultaneous trajectory tracking and tray orientation control. Existing methods often struggle with model dependency, uncertain parameters, and multi-object adaptability. We propose a shared teleoperation framework where humans and robots share positioning control, while the robot autonomously manages orientation to satisfy dynamic constraints. Key contributions include: 1) A theoretical dynamic constraint analysis utilizing a novel virtual object (VO)-based method to simplify constraints for trajectory planning. 2) An MPC-based trajectory smoothing algorithm that enforces real-time constraints and coordinates user tracking with orientation control. 3) Validations demonstrating stable manipulation of nine objects at accelerations up to 2.4 m/s2. Compared to the baseline, our approach reduces sliding distance by 72.45% and eliminates tip-overs (0% vs. 13.9%), proving robust adaptability in complex scenarios.

</details>


### [188] [Sustainable Transfer Learning for Adaptive Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06943)
*Khalil Abuibaid,Vinit Hegiste,Nigora Gafur,Achim Wagner,Martin Ruskowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在不同机器人平台之间进行策略迁移的可行性，重点关注使用强化学习完成插 peg-in-hole 任务。通过零样本迁移、微调和从头训练三种方式评估性能，结果表明微调能显著提升效率并减少训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高机器人技能学习的样本效率和可持续性，减少从头训练所需的时间和资源。

Method: 采用强化学习方法，在两个不同的机器人平台上进行策略训练，并测试零样本迁移、微调和从头训练三种策略迁移方式的效果。

Result: 零样本迁移成功率较低且执行时间较长，而微调显著提高了性能并减少了训练步数。

Conclusion: 结合适应技术的策略迁移可提升样本效率和泛化能力，有助于实现更可持续的机器人学习。

Abstract: Learning robot skills from scratch is often time-consuming, while reusing data promotes sustainability and improves sample efficiency. This study investigates policy transfer across different robotic platforms, focusing on peg-in-hole task using reinforcement learning (RL). Policy training is carried out on two different robots. Their policies are transferred and evaluated for zero-shot, fine-tuning, and training from scratch. Results indicate that zero-shot transfer leads to lower success rates and relatively longer task execution times, while fine-tuning significantly improves performance with fewer training time-steps. These findings highlight that policy transfer with adaptation techniques improves sample efficiency and generalization, reducing the need for extensive retraining and supporting sustainable robotic learning.

</details>


### [189] [Learning-Based Strategy for Composite Robot Assembly Skill Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06949)
*Khalil Abuibaid,Aleksandr Sidorenko,Achim Wagner,Martin Ruskowski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于残差强化学习（RRL）的可重用技能框架，用于解决位置控制机器人在接触丰富的peg-in-hole装配任务中的适应性问题，提升了鲁棒性与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于几何公差严格、摩擦变化和接触动力学不确定，传统工业机器人难以在接触丰富的操作中实现自适应，尤其在使用位置控制时。本文旨在通过结构化技能与学习相结合的方式解决该问题。

Method: 采用复合技能表示装配过程，包含明确的前置、后置和不变条件，并结合残差强化学习（RRL），仅对每个技能内部进行残差调整，保持整体技能结构不变。在MuJoCo仿真中使用UR5e机器人和SAC算法进行训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够在接触丰富的装配任务中实现鲁棒执行，具备良好的模块化、可重用性和安全性，同时提高了样本效率。

Conclusion: 所提出的技能封装与残差学习结合的方法适用于工业自动化场景，为位置控制机器人在复杂接触任务中的自适应提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich robotic skills remain challenging for industrial robots due to tight geometric tolerances, frictional variability, and uncertain contact dynamics, particularly when using position-controlled manipulators. This paper presents a reusable and encapsulated skill-based strategy for peg-in-hole assembly, in which adaptation is achieved through Residual Reinforcement Learning (RRL). The assembly process is represented using composite skills with explicit pre-, post-, and invariant conditions, enabling modularity, reusability, and well-defined execution semantics across task variations. Safety and sample efficiency are promoted through RRL by restricting adaptation to residual refinements within each skill during contact-rich interactions, while the overall skill structure and execution flow remain invariant. The proposed approach is evaluated in MuJoCo simulation on a UR5e robot equipped with a Robotiq gripper and trained using SAC and JAX. Results demonstrate that the proposed formulation enables robust execution of assembly skills, highlighting its suitability for industrial automation.

</details>


### [190] [Differentiable Environment-Trajectory Co-Optimization for Safe Multi-Agent Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06972)
*Zhan Gao,Gabriele Fadini,Stelian Coros,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双层优化框架，将环境配置作为决策变量与智能体动作共同优化，以实现安全导航。通过可微优化方法结合KKT条件和隐函数定理，实现了环境参数对智能体轨迹的梯度传播，并设计了新的导航安全性度量指标。实验表明该方法在多种安全关键场景中有效提升了导航的安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体导航方法将环境视为固定不变，忽略了环境配置对智能体性能的影响。本文旨在探索环境配置与智能体行为的联合优化，提升导航过程中的安全性和效率。

Method: 构建了一个双层优化问题：下层使用内点法优化智能体轨迹以最小化导航成本，上层使用梯度上升优化环境配置以最大化导航安全性。利用KKT条件和隐函数定理实现上下层之间的梯度传递，使整个双层结构可微。同时提出一种基于测度论验证的有效性导航安全度量指标。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的框架在仓库物流到城市交通等多种安全关键导航场景中均能有效提升安全性与效率。优化后的环境能够为智能体提供导航引导，降低碰撞风险并改善整体性能。此外，所提出的度量指标被证明具有理论有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将环境配置纳入联合优化过程，本文证明了环境设计在多智能体导航中的重要作用。所提出的可微双层优化方法能够有效协调环境与智能体行为，显著提升导航系统的安全性和效率，为未来智能交通和自动化系统的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: The environment plays a critical role in multi-agent navigation by imposing spatial constraints, rules, and limitations that agents must navigate around. Traditional approaches treat the environment as fixed, without exploring its impact on agents' performance. This work considers environment configurations as decision variables, alongside agent actions, to jointly achieve safe navigation. We formulate a bi-level problem, where the lower-level sub-problem optimizes agent trajectories that minimize navigation cost and the upper-level sub-problem optimizes environment configurations that maximize navigation safety. We develop a differentiable optimization method that iteratively solves the lower-level sub-problem with interior point methods and the upper-level sub-problem with gradient ascent. A key challenge lies in analytically coupling these two levels. We address this by leveraging KKT conditions and the Implicit Function Theorem to compute gradients of agent trajectories w.r.t. environment parameters, enabling differentiation throughout the bi-level structure. Moreover, we propose a novel metric that quantifies navigation safety as a criterion for the upper-level environment optimization, and prove its validity through measure theory. Our experiments validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework in a variety of safety-critical navigation scenarios, inspired from warehouse logistics to urban transportation. The results demonstrate that optimized environments provide navigation guidance, improving both agents' safety and efficiency.

</details>


### [191] [KITE: Keyframe-Indexed Tokenized Evidence for VLM-Based Robot Failure Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07034)
*Mehdi Hosseinzadeh,King Hang Wong,Feras Dayoub*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: KITE是一种无需训练的前端系统，通过关键帧和布局定位将机器人执行长视频转化为紧凑、可解释的视觉语言模型输入，支持故障检测、识别、定位、解释与修正，并在RoboFAC基准上显著优于基础VLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升视觉语言模型在机器人任务中对长时视频的理解能力，尤其是在无需额外训练的情况下实现高效、可解释的故障分析。

Method: KITE提取运动显著的关键帧，结合开放词汇检测与鸟瞰图（BEV）表示，编码对象布局、时间戳等信息，并与机器人和场景上下文令牌序列化为统一提示输入VLM。

Result: 在RoboFAC基准上，KITE配合Qwen2.5-VL显著提升了无训练设置下的故障检测、识别与定位性能，接近微调基线水平；QLoRA微调进一步改善了解释与修正效果，并在真实双臂机器人上验证了实用性。

Conclusion: KITE作为一种结构化、可解释的前端，能有效桥接机器人执行视频与视觉语言模型，适用于多种故障分析任务，具备良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We present KITE, a training-free, keyframe-anchored, layout-grounded front-end that converts long robot-execution videos into compact, interpretable tokenized evidence for vision-language models (VLMs). KITE distills each trajectory into a small set of motion-salient keyframes with open-vocabulary detections and pairs each keyframe with a schematic bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation that encodes relative object layout, axes, timestamps, and detection confidence. These visual cues are serialized with robot-profile and scene-context tokens into a unified prompt, allowing the same front-end to support failure detection, identification, localization, explanation, and correction with an off-the-shelf VLM. On the RoboFAC benchmark, KITE with Qwen2.5-VL substantially improves over vanilla Qwen2.5-VL in the training-free setting, with especially large gains on simulation failure detection, identification, and localization, while remaining competitive with a RoboFAC-tuned baseline. A small QLoRA fine-tune further improves explanation and correction quality. We also report qualitative results on real dual-arm robots, demonstrating the practical applicability of KITE as a structured and interpretable front-end for robot failure analysis. Code and models are released on our project page: https://m80hz.github.io/kite/

</details>


### [192] [Exploring the proprioceptive potential of joint receptors using a biomimetic robotic joint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07038)
*Akihiro Miki,Shun Hasegawa,Sota Yuzaki,Yuta Sahara,Yoshimoto Ribayashi,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究利用仿生机器人技术模拟I型关节感受器，发现其在屈曲和扭转运动中均能实现低于2度的本体感觉误差，提示关节感受器在本体感觉中的作用可能远超传统认知，并可能解释遗传性感觉自主神经病III型患者肘关节与膝关节本体感觉差异的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为肌肉纺锤体是关节角度感知的主要传感器，而关节感受器仅在极端角度起作用；然而关节感受器在关节囊中广泛分布，其在本体感觉中的真实作用尚不明确，因此需要重新评估其功能。

Method: 通过机器人技术构建仿生关节，模拟对缓慢持续运动响应的I型关节感受器，并量化其在关节弯曲和扭转运动中的本体感觉能力。

Result: I型类关节感受器单独即可实现平均误差小于2度的本体感觉，表明其具备高精度的位置感知能力。

Conclusion: 关节感受器在本体感觉中的作用被低估，其与肌肉纺锤体的贡献可能在发育和进化过程中被不同加权；该发现有助于理解特定神经病变中的本体感觉差异，并推动神经科学、医学与机器人学的交叉研究。

Abstract: In neuroscience, joint receptors have traditionally been viewed as limit detectors, providing positional information only at extreme joint angles, while muscle spindles are considered the primary sensors of joint angle position. However, joint receptors are widely distributed throughout the joint capsule, and their full role in proprioception remains unclear.
